\"selected_offer_ids\": list(selected_ids),\n                \"intervals\": 
intervals,\n                \"bid_cost_cents\": bid_cost,\n                
\"credit_cents\": credits,\n                \"grant_pool_cents\": pool,\n       
\"grant_funded_cents\": funded,\n                \"grant_allocated_cents\": 
allocated,\n                \"grant_unallocated_cents\": funded - allocated,\n  
\"grants\": [{\"beneficiary_id\": b, \"cents\": shares[b]} for b in 
sorted(shares)],\n                \"compliance_counts\": counts,\n              
\"offer_credits\": offer_credits,\n            }\n\n    return labels, results, 
stats, tie_decisions\n\n\nREGION_EVENT_FIELDS = (\n    
\"candidate_offer_count\",\n    \"selected_offer_ids\",\n    \"intervals\",\n   
\"bid_cost_cents\",\n    \"credit_cents\",\n    \"grant_pool_cents\",\n    
\"grant_funded_cents\",\n    \"grant_allocated_cents\",\n    
\"grant_unallocated_cents\",\n    \"grants\",\n    
\"compliance_counts\",\n)\n\nREGION_MONEY_FIELDS = (\n    \"bid_cost_cents\",\n 
\"credit_cents\",\n    \"grant_pool_cents\",\n    \"grant_funded_cents\",\n    
\"grant_allocated_cents\",\n    \"grant_unallocated_cents\",\n)\n\n\ndef 
region_document(ex, results, region):\n    events = []\n    totals = 
dict.fromkeys(\n        (\n            \"event_count\",\n            
\"candidate_offer_count\",\n            \"selected_offer_count\",\n            
\"relief_ticks\",\n            \"recovery_ticks\",\n            
\"shortfall_ticks\",\n            \"overdelivery_ticks\",\n        )\n        + 
REGION_MONEY_FIELDS,\n        0,\n    )\n    for event_id in 
ex.event_ids_sorted:\n        res = results[(event_id, region)]\n        entry =
{\"event_id\": event_id}\n        for field in REGION_EVENT_FIELDS:\n           
entry[field] = res[field]\n        events.append(entry)\n        
totals[\"event_count\"] += 1\n        totals[\"candidate_offer_count\"] += 
res[\"candidate_offer_count\"]\n        totals[\"selected_offer_count\"] += 
len(res[\"selected_offer_ids\"])\n        for interval in res[\"intervals\"]:\n 
for field in (\n                \"relief_ticks\",\n                
\"recovery_ticks\",\n                \"shortfall_ticks\",\n                
\"overdelivery_ticks\",\n            ):\n                totals[field] += 
interval[field]\n        for field in REGION_MONEY_FIELDS:\n            
totals[field] += res[field]\n    return {\"region\": region, \"events\": events,
\"totals\": totals}\n\n\ndef segment_document(ex, results, segment):\n    
entries = []\n    totals = dict.fromkeys(\n        (\n            
\"selected_offer_count\",\n            \"relief_ticks\",\n            
\"recovery_ticks\",\n            \"bid_cost_cents\",\n            
\"credit_cents\",\n            \"floor_ticks\",\n        ),\n        0,\n    )\n
for event_id in ex.event_ids_sorted:\n        for region in ex.region_names:\n  
res = results[(event_id, region)]\n            offer_ids = []\n            
relief = 0\n            recovery = 0\n            bid_cost = 0\n            
credits = 0\n            for offer_id in res[\"selected_offer_ids\"]:\n         
offer = ex.offers[offer_id]\n                if offer[\"segment\"] != segment:\n
continue\n                offer_ids.append(offer_id)\n                relief += 
offer[\"total_relief\"]\n                recovery += offer[\"total_recovery\"]\n
bid_cost += offer[\"bid_cost_cents\"]\n                credits += 
res[\"offer_credits\"][offer_id]\n            floor = ex.floors.get((event_id, 
region, segment), (0, 0))[0]\n            attainment = 10000 if floor == 0 else 
min(10000, round_half_up(10000 * relief, floor))\n            entries.append(\n 
{\n                    \"event_id\": event_id,\n                    \"region\": 
region,\n                    \"selected_offer_ids\": offer_ids,\n               
\"relief_ticks\": relief,\n                    \"recovery_ticks\": recovery,\n  
\"bid_cost_cents\": bid_cost,\n                    \"credit_cents\": credits,\n 
\"floor_ticks\": floor,\n                    \"floor_attainment_bp\": 
attainment,\n                }\n            )\n            
totals[\"selected_offer_count\"] += len(offer_ids)\n            
totals[\"relief_ticks\"] += relief\n            totals[\"recovery_ticks\"] += 
recovery\n            totals[\"bid_cost_cents\"] += bid_cost\n            
totals[\"credit_cents\"] += credits\n            totals[\"floor_ticks\"] += 
floor\n    return {\"segment\": segment, \"event_regions\": entries, \"totals\":
totals}\n\n\ndef summary_document(ex, region_docs, segment_docs):\n    summed = 
(\n        \"candidate_offer_count\",\n        \"selected_offer_count\",\n      
\"relief_ticks\",\n        \"recovery_ticks\",\n        \"shortfall_ticks\",\n  
\"overdelivery_ticks\",\n    ) + REGION_MONEY_FIELDS\n    document = 
dict.fromkeys(summed, 0)\n    document[\"event_count\"] = len(ex.events)\n    
document[\"event_region_count\"] = len(ex.events) * len(ex.region_names)\n    
document[\"site_count\"] = len(ex.site_rows)\n    regions = {}\n    for region, 
doc in region_docs.items():\n        totals = doc[\"totals\"]\n        for field
in summed:\n            document[field] += totals[field]\n        
regions[region] = {\n            \"selected_offer_count\": 
totals[\"selected_offer_count\"],\n            \"relief_ticks\": 
totals[\"relief_ticks\"],\n            \"bid_cost_cents\": 
totals[\"bid_cost_cents\"],\n            \"credit_cents\": 
totals[\"credit_cents\"],\n            \"grant_allocated_cents\": 
totals[\"grant_allocated_cents\"],\n        }\n    segments = {}\n    for 
segment, doc in segment_docs.items():\n        totals = doc[\"totals\"]\n       
segments[segment] = {\n            \"selected_offer_count\": 
totals[\"selected_offer_count\"],\n            \"relief_ticks\": 
totals[\"relief_ticks\"],\n            \"bid_cost_cents\": 
totals[\"bid_cost_cents\"],\n            \"credit_cents\": 
totals[\"credit_cents\"],\n        }\n    document[\"regions\"] = regions\n    
document[\"segments\"] = segments\n    return document\n\n\ndef 
data_quality_document(ex, stats):\n    zero_floor = 0\n    for event_id in 
ex.events:\n        for region in ex.region_names:\n            for segment in 
ex.segment_names:\n                if ex.floors.get((event_id, region, segment),
(0, 0))[0] == 0:\n                    zero_floor += 1\n    return {\n        
\"rows\": {\n            \"events.json\": ex.event_rows,\n            
\"sites.csv\": ex.sites_rows,\n            \"offers.csv\": ex.offers_rows,\n    
\"profiles.tsv\": ex.profiles_rows,\n            \"targets.tsv\": 
ex.targets_rows,\n            \"continuity_waivers.csv\": ex.waiver_rows,\n     
\"recipient_weights.tsv\": ex.weight_rows,\n            \"site_allowances.tsv\":
ex.allowance_rows,\n            \"feeder_limits.txt\": ex.feeder_rows,\n        
\"pools.txt\": ex.pool_rows,\n            \"site_state.jsonl\": ex.state_rows,\n
\"segment_floors.json\": ex.floor_rows,\n            \"rate_cards.xml\": 
ex.card_rows,\n            \"export_manifest.tsv\": ex.manifest_rows,\n        
},\n        \"orphan_offer_rows\": ex.orphan_offer_rows,\n        
\"orphan_profile_rows\": ex.orphan_profile_rows,\n        
\"out_of_range_profile_rows\": ex.out_of_range_profile_rows,\n        
\"duplicate_profile_rows\": ex.duplicate_profile_rows,\n        
\"unknown_region_site_rows\": ex.unknown_region_site_rows,\n        
\"waiver_signed_missing\": ex.waiver_signed_missing,\n        
\"ineligible_offer_count\": stats[\"ineligible_offer_count\"],\n        
\"allowance_blocked_offer_count\": stats[\"allowance_blocked_offer_count\"],\n  
\"empty_candidate_groups\": stats[\"empty_candidate_groups\"],\n        
\"zero_floor_count\": zero_floor,\n        \"unallocated_pool_count\": 
stats[\"unallocated_pool_count\"],\n    }\n\n\ndef policy_audit_document(labels,
stats, tie_decisions):\n    counts = dict.fromkeys(LABELS, 0)\n    site_labels =
[]\n    for key in sorted(labels):\n        counts[labels[key]] += 1\n        
site_labels.append({\"event_id\": key[0], \"site_id\": key[1], \"label\": 
labels[key]})\n    return {\n        \"compliance_counts\": counts,\n        
\"site_labels\": site_labels,\n        \"tie_decisions\": sorted(tie_decisions, 
key=lambda d: (d[\"event_id\"], d[\"region\"])),\n        \"cap_binding_count\":
stats[\"cap_binding_count\"],\n        \"residual_cents\": 
stats[\"residual_cents\"],\n        \"residual_tie_pool_count\": 
stats[\"residual_tie_pool_count\"],\n    }\n\n\ndef write_json(out_dir, name, 
document):\n    with open(os.path.join(out_dir, name), \"w\", 
encoding=\"utf-8\") as fh:\n        json.dump(document, fh, sort_keys=True, 
indent=1)\n        fh.write(\"\\n\")\n\n\ndef main(argv):\n    if len(argv) != 
3:\n        print(\"usage: derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR\", 
file=sys.stderr)\n        return 2\n    data_dir, out_dir = argv[1], argv[2]\n  
os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    ex = Extract(data_dir)\n    labels, 
results, stats, tie_decisions = run(ex)\n\n    region_docs = {region: 
region_document(ex, results, region) for region in ex.region_names}\n    
segment_docs = {seg: segment_document(ex, results, seg) for seg in 
ex.segment_names}\n    for region, doc in region_docs.items():\n        
write_json(out_dir, \"region_%s.json\" % region, doc)\n    for segment, doc in 
segment_docs.items():\n        write_json(out_dir, \"segment_%s.json\" % 
segment, doc)\n    write_json(out_dir, \"summary.json\", summary_document(ex, 
region_docs, segment_docs))\n    write_json(out_dir, \"policy_audit.json\", 
policy_audit_document(labels, stats, tie_decisions))\n    write_json(out_dir, 
\"data_quality.json\", data_quality_document(ex, stats))\n    return 0\n\n\nif 
__name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))\n"}

Reasoning:
Before recreating the file, I want to double-check a few details: the floor 
filtering logic in solve_portfolio only keeps entries with positive floor and 
priority since others contribute zero to deficit, offer_credits is correctly 
excluded from the region document since that builds its own separate dict, and 
I'm verifying the summary_document logic that iterates fields against totals to 
make sure it's safe against mutation while iterating.

I'm also confirming event_count stays correct since it's excluded from totals 
summation, that allowance consumption updates happen after candidates are 
computed at event start so remaining values aren't affected mid-loop, that 
carried cents propagate correctly across events in season order, and that the 
funded pool calculation for grant plus carried cents minus credit cents clamped 
at zero is right.

Checking remaining smaller details: site rows are unique per event so no 
duplicate region offers within the same event, data_quality row counts for 
site_allowances exclude the header line correctly, offer_events includes all 
offers even orphans for the profile range check, and the NegStr class resolves 
fine at runtime despite being defined after the Extract class since load_events 
is only called once the module is fully loaded. I also verify recursion depth in
walk stays small, offer_count is passed correctly as len(chosen), and 
tie-breaking for best identifiers uses sorted tuple comparison as specified.

I confirm segment recovery sums offer recoveries correctly per interval, JSON 
output uses indent=1 with sorted keys, and summary totals naturally match region
file sums by construction. Policy audit tie_decisions get sorted by event_id and
region, and empty_candidate_groups correctly counts event-regions lacking any 
candidate offer.

Now I'm worried that computing rates for every site row could crash with a 
KeyError if a card is missing for some site — so I'm restructuring to compute 
rates lazily only for chosen offers' site rows, caching the lookup per offer.

I also decide missing dispatch_authorized state should default to ineligible, 
and I'm planning to precompute each offer's segment at load time rather than 
re-deriving it repeatedly inside the walk function, to avoid redundant lookups.



Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/out/derive_report.py"
  file_text: 
    #!/usr/bin/env python3
    """Derive the district-heat flexibility programme reports from a season 
extract.
    
    Usage: python3 derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR
    """
    
    import configparser
    import csv
    import json
    import os
    import sys
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    from datetime import datetime
    
    LABELS = ["SAFETY_HOLD", "COOLDOWN", "CONTINUITY_WAIVED", 
"CERTIFICATE_PENDING", "CLEAR"]
    
    
    def round_half_up(num, den):
        return (2 * num + den) // (2 * den)
    
    
    def round_half_even(num, den):
        q, r = divmod(num, den)
        twice = 2 * r
        if twice > den or (twice == den and q % 2):
            q += 1
        return q
    
    
    def read_rows(path, delimiter):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
            return list(csv.DictReader(fh, delimiter=delimiter))
    
    
    def non_comment_lines(path):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            return [line.rstrip("\n") for line in fh if not 
line.startswith("#")]
    
    
    class NegStr:
        """Sort helper giving descending string order inside an ascending sort 
key."""
    
        __slots__ = ("value",)
    
        def __init__(self, value):
            self.value = value
    
        def __lt__(self, other):
            return self.value > other.value
    
        def __eq__(self, other):
            return self.value == other.value
    
    
    class Extract:
        def __init__(self, data_dir):
            self.dir = data_dir
            self.load_program()
            self.load_events()
            self.load_sites()
            self.load_offers()
            self.load_profiles()
            self.load_targets()
            self.load_feeder_limits()
            self.load_state()
            self.load_waivers()
            self.load_floors()
            self.load_rate_cards()
            self.load_weights()
            self.load_pools()
            self.load_allowances()
            self.load_manifest()
    
        def path(self, name):
            return os.path.join(self.dir, name)
    
        def ticks(self, wh):
            return round_half_up(wh, self.wh_per_tick)
    
        def load_program(self):
            cp = configparser.ConfigParser()
            with open(self.path("program.ini"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                cp.read_file(fh)
            self.wh_per_tick = int(cp["units"]["wh_per_tick"])
            self.millicents_per_cent = int(cp["units"]["millicents_per_cent"])
            self.region_of_code = {k.upper(): v for k, v in 
cp["region_codes"].items()}
            self.segment_of_code = {k.upper(): v for k, v in 
cp["segment_codes"].items()}
            self.region_names = sorted(set(self.region_of_code.values()))
            self.segment_names = sorted(set(self.segment_of_code.values()))
            self.segment_adder = {name: 0 for name in self.segment_names}
            if cp.has_section("segment_rate_adders"):
                by_lower = {name.lower(): name for name in self.segment_names}
                for key, value in cp["segment_rate_adders"].items():
                    name = by_lower.get(key.lower())
                    if name is not None:
                        self.segment_adder[name] = int(value)
            self.claim_cents_per_weight = 
int(cp["grants"]["claim_cents_per_weight"])
            self.minimum_grant_cents = int(cp["grants"]["minimum_grant_cents"])
    
        def load_events(self):
            with open(self.path("events.json"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                events = json.load(fh)
            self.event_rows = len(events)
            self.events = {}
            for ev in events:
                self.events[ev["event_id"]] = {
                    "event_id": ev["event_id"],
                    "interval_count": int(ev["interval_count"]),
                    "cost_weight": int(ev["objective"]["cost_weight"]),
                    "recovery_weight": int(ev["objective"]["recovery_weight"]),
                    "uplift": int(ev["uplift_millicents_per_kwh"]),
                    "start": datetime.fromisoformat(ev["dispatch_start"]),
                }
            self.event_ids_sorted = sorted(self.events)
            self.season_order = sorted(
                self.events.values(), key=lambda e: (e["start"], 
NegStr(e["event_id"]))
            )
    
        def load_sites(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("sites.csv"), ",")
            self.sites_rows = len(rows)
            self.unknown_region_site_rows = 0
            self.site_rows = {}
            for row in rows:
                region = self.region_of_code.get(row["region_code"])
                if region is None:
                    self.unknown_region_site_rows += 1
                    continue
                self.site_rows[(row["event_id"], row["site_id"])] = {
                    "event_id": row["event_id"],
                    "site_id": row["site_id"],
                    "region": region,
                    "segment": self.segment_of_code[row["segment_code"]],
                    "beneficiary_id": row["beneficiary_id"],
                    "critical": row["critical"] == "Y",
                }
    
        def load_offers(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("offers.csv"), ",")
            self.offers_rows = len(rows)
            self.orphan_offer_rows = 0
            self.offers = {}
            self.offer_events = {}
            for row in rows:
                self.offer_events[row["offer_id"]] = row["event_id"]
                site = self.site_rows.get((row["event_id"], row["site_id"]))
                if site is None:
                    self.orphan_offer_rows += 1
                    continue
                self.offers[row["offer_id"]] = {
                    "offer_id": row["offer_id"],
                    "event_id": row["event_id"],
                    "site_id": row["site_id"],
                    "segment": site["segment"],
                    "bid_cost_cents": int(row["bid_cost_cents"]),
                }
    
        def load_profiles(self):
            self.profiles_rows = 0
            self.orphan_profile_rows = 0
            self.out_of_range_profile_rows = 0
            self.duplicate_profile_rows = 0
            relief = {}
            recovery = {}
            seen = set()
            with open(self.path("profiles.tsv"), encoding="utf-8", newline="") 
as fh:
                for row in csv.DictReader(fh, delimiter="\t"):
                    self.profiles_rows += 1
                    offer_id = row["offer_id"]
                    event_id = self.offer_events.get(offer_id)
                    if event_id is None:
                        self.orphan_profile_rows += 1
                        continue
                    interval = int(row["interval"])
                    if interval < 1 or interval > 
self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]:
                        self.out_of_range_profile_rows += 1
                        continue
                    key = (offer_id, interval)
                    if key in seen:
                        self.duplicate_profile_rows += 1
                        continue
                    seen.add(key)
                    if offer_id not in self.offers:
                        continue
                    mult = int(row["register_multiplier"])
                    relief[key] = self.ticks(int(row["relief_wh"]) * mult)
                    recovery[key] = self.ticks(int(row["recovery_wh"]) * mult)
            for offer in self.offers.values():
                offer_id = offer["offer_id"]
                count = self.events[offer["event_id"]]["interval_count"]
                offer["relief"] = [relief.get((offer_id, t), 0) for t in 
range(1, count + 1)]
                offer["recovery"] = [recovery.get((offer_id, t), 0) for t in 
range(1, count + 1)]
                offer["total_relief"] = sum(offer["relief"])
                offer["total_recovery"] = sum(offer["recovery"])
    
        def load_targets(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("targets.tsv"), "\t")
            self.targets_rows = len(rows)
            self.targets = {}
            for row in rows:
                region = self.region_of_code[row["region_code"]]
                self.targets[(row["event_id"], region, int(row["interval"]))] = 
(
                    self.ticks(int(row["target_wh"])),
                    int(row["shortfall_weight"]),
                )
    
        def load_feeder_limits(self):
            lines = non_comment_lines(self.path("feeder_limits.txt"))
            self.feeder_rows = len(lines)
            self.ceilings = {}
            for line in lines:
                region = self.region_of_code[line[10:14].strip()]
                key = (line[0:10].strip(), region, int(line[14:17]))
                self.ceilings[key] = (self.ticks(int(line[17:27])), 
self.ticks(int(line[27:37])))
    
        def load_state(self):
            self.state_rows = 0
            self.state = {}
            with open(self.path("site_state.jsonl"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                for line in fh:
                    if not line.strip():
                        continue
                    self.state_rows += 1
                    obj = json.loads(line)
                    key = (obj["event_id"], obj["site_id"])
                    if key in self.site_rows:
                        self.state[key] = obj
    
        def load_waivers(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("continuity_waivers.csv"), ",")
            self.waiver_rows = len(rows)
            self.waiver_signed_missing = 0
            self.waivers = {}
            for row in rows:
                if (row.get("waiver_signed") or "").strip() == "":
                    self.waiver_signed_missing += 1
                key = (row["event_id"], row["site_id"])
                if key in self.site_rows:
                    self.waivers[key] = row
    
        def load_floors(self):
            with open(self.path("segment_floors.json"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                entries = json.load(fh)["floors"]
            self.floor_rows = len(entries)
            self.floors = {}
            for entry in entries:
                key = (
                    entry["event_id"],
                    self.region_of_code[entry["region_code"]],
                    self.segment_of_code[entry["segment_code"]],
                )
                self.floors[key] = (self.ticks(int(entry["floor_wh"])), 
int(entry["priority_weight"]))
    
        def load_rate_cards(self):
            cards = 
ET.parse(self.path("rate_cards.xml")).getroot().findall("card")
            self.card_rows = len(cards)
            self.cards = {}
            for card in cards:
                key = (card.get("event"), card.get("site"))
                if key not in self.site_rows:
                    continue
                override = card.find("override")
                source = override if override is not None else card.find("base")
                self.cards[key] = (int(source.text), int(card.find("cap").text))
    
        def load_weights(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("recipient_weights.tsv"), "\t")
            self.weight_rows = len(rows)
            self.grant_weights = {}
            for row in rows:
                key = (row["event_id"], row["site_id"])
                if key in self.site_rows:
                    self.grant_weights[key] = int(row["grant_weight"])
    
        def load_pools(self):
            lines = non_comment_lines(self.path("pools.txt"))
            self.pool_rows = len(lines)
            self.pools = {}
            for line in lines:
                region = self.region_of_code[line[10:14].strip()]
                self.pools[(line[0:10].strip(), region)] = int(line[14:26])
    
        def load_allowances(self):
            lines = non_comment_lines(self.path("site_allowances.tsv"))
            self.allowance_rows = max(0, len(lines) - 1)
            self.allowance_ticks = {}
            draws_from = {}
            for line in lines[1:]:
                fields = line.split("\t")
                self.allowance_ticks[fields[0]] = int(fields[1])
                draws_from[fields[0]] = fields[2].strip() if len(fields) > 2 
else ""
            self.allowance_root = {}
            for site_id in self.allowance_ticks:
                node = site_id
                while draws_from.get(node):
                    node = draws_from[node]
                self.allowance_root[site_id] = node
    
        def load_manifest(self):
            self.manifest_rows = max(0, 
len(non_comment_lines(self.path("export_manifest.tsv"))) - 1)
    
        def compliance_label(self, key):
            state = self.state.get(key, {})
            if state.get("safety_hold"):
                return "SAFETY_HOLD"
            if state.get("cooldown"):
                return "COOLDOWN"
            waiver = self.waivers.get(key, {})
            if waiver.get("routine_certificate_pending") != "Y":
                return "CLEAR"
            site = self.site_rows[key]
            valid = (
                waiver.get("waiver_status") == "VALID"
                and waiver.get("waiver_signed") == "Y"
                and site["critical"]
                and site["segment"] == "public"
            )
            return "CONTINUITY_WAIVED" if valid else "CERTIFICATE_PENDING"
    
        def rates(self, key):
            """Return (pre-cap rate, effective rate, cap binding) for a site 
row."""
            start, cap = self.cards[key]
            site = self.site_rows[key]
            pre_cap = start + self.events[site["event_id"]]["uplift"] + 
self.segment_adder[site["segment"]]
            return pre_cap, min(pre_cap, cap), pre_cap > cap
    
        def credit_cents(self, offer, rate):
            den = 1000 * self.millicents_per_cent
            total = 0
            for relief, recovery in zip(offer["relief"], offer["recovery"]):
                credited = relief - recovery
                if credited > 0:
                    total += round_half_even(credited * self.wh_per_tick * rate,
den)
            return total
    
    
    def solve_portfolio(ex, event, region, site_groups):
        """Return (best portfolio offer ids, number of portfolios tied at the 
best value)."""
        count = event["interval_count"]
        event_id = event["event_id"]
        targets = []
        weights = []
        relief_cap = []
        recovery_cap = []
        for t in range(1, count + 1):
            target, weight = ex.targets.get((event_id, region, t), (0, 0))
            targets.append(target)
            weights.append(weight)
            rcap, vcap = ex.ceilings.get((event_id, region, t), (0, 0))
            relief_cap.append(rcap)
            recovery_cap.append(vcap)
        floors = []
        for segment in ex.segment_names:
            floor, priority = ex.floors.get((event_id, region, segment), (0, 0))
            if floor > 0 and priority > 0:
                floors.append((segment, floor, priority))
        cost_w = event["cost_weight"]
        rec_w = event["recovery_weight"]
    
        group_offers = [offers for _, offers in sorted(site_groups.items())]
        n = len(group_offers)
        relief = [0] * count
        recovery = [0] * count
        seg_relief = {segment: 0 for segment in ex.segment_names}
        chosen = []
        best_key = None
        best_ids = None
        tied = 0
    
        def leaf(cost):
            nonlocal best_key, best_ids, tied
            shortfall = 0
            over = 0
            recovery_total = 0
            for t in range(count):
                diff = targets[t] - relief[t]
                if diff > 0:
                    shortfall += weights[t] * diff
                else:
                    over -= diff
                recovery_total += recovery[t]
            deficit = 0
            for segment, floor, priority in floors:
                gap = floor - seg_relief[segment]
                if gap > 0:
                    deficit += priority * gap
            key = (shortfall, deficit, cost_w * cost + rec_w * recovery_total, 
over, len(chosen))
            if best_key is None or key < best_key:
                best_key = key
                best_ids = tuple(sorted(chosen))
                tied = 1
            elif key == best_key:
                tied += 1
                ids = tuple(sorted(chosen))
                if ids > best_ids:
                    best_ids = ids
    
        def walk(index, cost):
            if index == n:
                leaf(cost)
                return
            walk(index + 1, cost)
            for offer in group_offers[index]:
                orelief = offer["relief"]
                orecovery = offer["recovery"]
                feasible = True
                for t in range(count):
                    if (
                        relief[t] + orelief[t] > relief_cap[t]
                        or recovery[t] + orecovery[t] > recovery_cap[t]
                    ):
                        feasible = False
                        break
                if not feasible:
                    continue
                for t in range(count):
                    relief[t] += orelief[t]
                    recovery[t] += orecovery[t]
                seg_relief[offer["segment"]] += offer["total_relief"]
                chosen.append(offer["offer_id"])
                walk(index + 1, cost + offer["bid_cost_cents"])
                chosen.pop()
                seg_relief[offer["segment"]] -= offer["total_relief"]
                for t in range(count):
                    relief[t] -= orelief[t]
                    recovery[t] -= orecovery[t]
    
        walk(0, 0)
        return best_ids, tied
    
    
    def largest_remainder(funded, weights):
        """Share `funded` cents by largest remainder. Returns (shares, residual,
tie)."""
        total_weight = sum(weights.values())
        shares = {}
        remainders = {}
        for beneficiary, weight in weights.items():
            shares[beneficiary], remainders[beneficiary] = divmod(funded * 
weight, total_weight)
        leftover = funded - sum(shares.values())
        order = sorted(remainders, key=lambda b: (remainders[b], b), 
reverse=True)
        for beneficiary in order[:leftover]:
            shares[beneficiary] += 1
        seen = set()
        tie = False
        for value in remainders.values():
            if value:
                if value in seen:
                    tie = True
                    break
                seen.add(value)
        return shares, leftover, tie
    
    
    def apportion(ex, funded, weights):
        """Share a funded pool among beneficiaries. Returns (shares, residual 
cents, tie)."""
        positive = {b: w for b, w in weights.items() if w > 0}
        if not positive:
            return {}, 0, False
        claims = {b: w * ex.claim_cents_per_weight for b, w in positive.items()}
        if sum(claims.values()) <= funded:
            return claims, 0, False
        shares, residual, tie = largest_remainder(funded, positive)
        kept = {b: w for b, w in positive.items() if shares[b] >= 
ex.minimum_grant_cents}
        if not kept:
            return {b: 0 for b in positive}, 0, False
        if len(kept) == len(positive):
            return shares, residual, tie
        second, residual, tie = largest_remainder(funded, kept)
        return {b: second.get(b, 0) for b in positive}, residual, tie
    
    
    def run(ex):
        labels = {key: ex.compliance_label(key) for key in ex.site_rows}
    
        offers_by_event = {}
        for offer in ex.offers.values():
            offers_by_event.setdefault(offer["event_id"], []).append(offer)
        site_rows_by_event_region = {}
        for key, site in ex.site_rows.items():
            site_rows_by_event_region.setdefault((site["event_id"], 
site["region"]), []).append(key)
    
        stats = {
            "ineligible_offer_count": 0,
            "allowance_blocked_offer_count": 0,
            "empty_candidate_groups": 0,
            "cap_binding_count": 0,
            "residual_cents": 0,
            "residual_tie_pool_count": 0,
            "unallocated_pool_count": 0,
        }
        consumed = {}
        carried = {region: 0 for region in ex.region_names}
        results = {}
        tie_decisions = []
    
        for event in ex.season_order:
            event_id = event["event_id"]
            count = event["interval_count"]
            candidates = {}
            for offer in offers_by_event.get(event_id, []):
                key = (event_id, offer["site_id"])
                if not ex.state.get(key, {}).get("dispatch_authorized"):
                    stats["ineligible_offer_count"] += 1
                    continue
                root = ex.allowance_root[offer["site_id"]]
                if offer["total_relief"] > ex.allowance_ticks[root] - 
consumed.get(root, 0):
                    stats["allowance_blocked_offer_count"] += 1
                    continue
                region = ex.site_rows[key]["region"]
                candidates.setdefault(region, {}).setdefault(offer["site_id"], 
[]).append(offer)
    
            for region in ex.region_names:
                site_groups = candidates.get(region, {})
                candidate_count = sum(len(group) for group in 
site_groups.values())
                if candidate_count == 0:
                    stats["empty_candidate_groups"] += 1
                selected_ids, tied = solve_portfolio(ex, event, region, 
site_groups)
                if tied >= 2:
                    tie_decisions.append(
                        {
                            "event_id": event_id,
                            "region": region,
                            "tied_portfolio_count": tied,
                            "selected_offer_ids": list(selected_ids),
                        }
                    )
    
                relief = [0] * count
                recovery = [0] * count
                bid_cost = 0
                credits = 0
                offer_credits = {}
                for offer_id in selected_ids:
                    offer = ex.offers[offer_id]
                    key = (event_id, offer["site_id"])
                    _, rate, binding = ex.rates(key)
                    if binding:
                        stats["cap_binding_count"] += 1
                    offer_credits[offer_id] = ex.credit_cents(offer, rate)
                    credits += offer_credits[offer_id]
                    bid_cost += offer["bid_cost_cents"]
                    for t in range(count):
                        relief[t] += offer["relief"][t]
                        recovery[t] += offer["recovery"][t]
                    root = ex.allowance_root[offer["site_id"]]
                    consumed[root] = consumed.get(root, 0) + 
offer["total_relief"]
    
                intervals = []
                for t in range(count):
                    target = ex.targets.get((event_id, region, t + 1), (0, 
0))[0]
                    intervals.append(
                        {
                            "interval": t + 1,
                            "target_ticks": target,
                            "relief_ticks": relief[t],
                            "recovery_ticks": recovery[t],
                            "shortfall_ticks": max(0, target - relief[t]),
                            "overdelivery_ticks": max(0, relief[t] - target),
                        }
                    )
    
                pool = ex.pools.get((event_id, region), 0)
                funded = max(0, pool + carried[region] - credits)
                site_keys = site_rows_by_event_region.get((event_id, region), 
[])
                weights = {}
                recipients = 0
                for key in site_keys:
                    if key not in ex.grant_weights:
                        continue
                    recipients += 1
                    weight = ex.grant_weights[key]
                    if labels[key] == "CONTINUITY_WAIVED":
                        weight *= 2
                    beneficiary = ex.site_rows[key]["beneficiary_id"]
                    weights[beneficiary] = weights.get(beneficiary, 0) + weight
                if recipients == 0 or sum(weights.values()) == 0:
                    stats["unallocated_pool_count"] += 1
                    shares, residual, tie = {}, 0, False
                else:
                    shares, residual, tie = apportion(ex, funded, weights)
                stats["residual_cents"] += residual
                if tie:
                    stats["residual_tie_pool_count"] += 1
                allocated = sum(shares.values())
                carried[region] = funded - allocated
    
                counts = {label: 0 for label in LABELS}
                for key in site_keys:
                    counts[labels[key]] += 1
    
                results[(event_id, region)] = {
                    "candidate_offer_count": candidate_count,
                    "selected_offer_ids": list(selected_ids),
                    "intervals": intervals,
                    "bid_cost_cents": bid_cost,
                    "credit_cents": credits,
                    "grant_pool_cents": pool,
                    "grant_funded_cents": funded,
                    "grant_allocated_cents": allocated,
                    "grant_unallocated_cents": funded - allocated,
                    "grants": [{"beneficiary_id": b, "cents": shares[b]} for b 
in sorted(shares)],
                    "compliance_counts": counts,
                    "offer_credits": offer_credits,
                }
    
        return labels, results, stats, tie_decisions
    
    
    REGION_EVENT_FIELDS = (
        "candidate_offer_count",
        "selected_offer_ids",
        "intervals",
        "bid_cost_cents",
        "credit_cents",
        "grant_pool_cents",
        "grant_funded_cents",
        "grant_allocated_cents",
        "grant_unallocated_cents",
        "grants",
        "compliance_counts",
    )
    
    REGION_MONEY_FIELDS = (
        "bid_cost_cents",
        "credit_cents",
        "grant_pool_cents",
        "grant_funded_cents",
        "grant_allocated_cents",
        "grant_unallocated_cents",
    )
    
    
    def region_document(ex, results, region):
        events = []
        totals = dict.fromkeys(
            (
                "event_count",
                "candidate_offer_count",
                "selected_offer_count",
                "relief_ticks",
                "recovery_ticks",
                "shortfall_ticks",
                "overdelivery_ticks",
            )
            + REGION_MONEY_FIELDS,
            0,
        )
        for event_id in ex.event_ids_sorted:
            res = results[(event_id, region)]
            entry = {"event_id": event_id}
            for field in REGION_EVENT_FIELDS:
                entry[field] = res[field]
            events.append(entry)
            totals["event_count"] += 1
            totals["candidate_offer_count"] += res["candidate_offer_count"]
            totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(res["selected_offer_ids"])
            for interval in res["intervals"]:
                for field in (
                    "relief_ticks",
                    "recovery_ticks",
                    "shortfall_ticks",
                    "overdelivery_ticks",
                ):
                    totals[field] += interval[field]
            for field in REGION_MONEY_FIELDS:
                totals[field] += res[field]
        return {"region": region, "events": events, "totals": totals}
    
    
    def segment_document(ex, results, segment):
        entries = []
        totals = dict.fromkeys(
            (
                "selected_offer_count",
                "relief_ticks",
                "recovery_ticks",
                "bid_cost_cents",
                "credit_cents",
                "floor_ticks",
            ),
            0,
        )
        for event_id in ex.event_ids_sorted:
            for region in ex.region_names:
                res = results[(event_id, region)]
                offer_ids = []
                relief = 0
                recovery = 0
                bid_cost = 0
                credits = 0
                for offer_id in res["selected_offer_ids"]:
                    offer = ex.offers[offer_id]
                    if offer["segment"] != segment:
                        continue
                    offer_ids.append(offer_id)
                    relief += offer["total_relief"]
                    recovery += offer["total_recovery"]
                    bid_cost += offer["bid_cost_cents"]
                    credits += res["offer_credits"][offer_id]
                floor = ex.floors.get((event_id, region, segment), (0, 0))[0]
                attainment = 10000 if floor == 0 else min(10000, 
round_half_up(10000 * relief, floor))
                entries.append(
                    {
                        "event_id": event_id,
                        "region": region,
                        "selected_offer_ids": offer_ids,
                        "relief_ticks": relief,
                        "recovery_ticks": recovery,
                        "bid_cost_cents": bid_cost,
                        "credit_cents": credits,
                        "floor_ticks": floor,
                        "floor_attainment_bp": attainment,
                    }
                )
                totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(offer_ids)
                totals["relief_ticks"] += relief
                totals["recovery_ticks"] += recovery
                totals["bid_cost_cents"] += bid_cost
                totals["credit_cents"] += credits
                totals["floor_ticks"] += floor
        return {"segment": segment, "event_regions": entries, "totals": totals}
    
    
    def summary_document(ex, region_docs, segment_docs):
        summed = (
            "candidate_offer_count",
            "selected_offer_count",
            "relief_ticks",
            "recovery_ticks",
            "shortfall_ticks",
            "overdelivery_ticks",
        ) + REGION_MONEY_FIELDS
        document = dict.fromkeys(summed, 0)
        document["event_count"] = len(ex.events)
        document["event_region_count"] = len(ex.events) * len(ex.region_names)
        document["site_count"] = len(ex.site_rows)
        regions = {}
        for region, doc in region_docs.items():
            totals = doc["totals"]
            for field in summed:
                document[field] += totals[field]
            regions[region] = {
                "selected_offer_count": totals["selected_offer_count"],
                "relief_ticks": totals["relief_ticks"],
                "bid_cost_cents": totals["bid_cost_cents"],
                "credit_cents": totals["credit_cents"],
                "grant_allocated_cents": totals["grant_allocated_cents"],
            }
        segments = {}
        for segment, doc in segment_docs.items():
            totals = doc["totals"]
            segments[segment] = {
                "selected_offer_count": totals["selected_offer_count"],
                "relief_ticks": totals["relief_ticks"],
                "bid_cost_cents": totals["bid_cost_cents"],
                "credit_cents": totals["credit_cents"],
            }
        document["regions"] = regions
        document["segments"] = segments
        return document
    
    
    def data_quality_document(ex, stats):
        zero_floor = 0
        for event_id in ex.events:
            for region in ex.region_names:
                for segment in ex.segment_names:
                    if ex.floors.get((event_id, region, segment), (0, 0))[0] == 
0:
                        zero_floor += 1
        return {
            "rows": {
                "events.json": ex.event_rows,
                "sites.csv": ex.sites_rows,
                "offers.csv": ex.offers_rows,
                "profiles.tsv": ex.profiles_rows,
                "targets.tsv": ex.targets_rows,
                "continuity_waivers.csv": ex.waiver_rows,
                "recipient_weights.tsv": ex.weight_rows,
                "site_allowances.tsv": ex.allowance_rows,
                "feeder_limits.txt": ex.feeder_rows,
                "pools.txt": ex.pool_rows,
                "site_state.jsonl": ex.state_rows,
                "segment_floors.json": ex.floor_rows,
                "rate_cards.xml": ex.card_rows,
                "export_manifest.tsv": ex.manifest_rows,
            },
            "orphan_offer_rows": ex.orphan_offer_rows,
            "orphan_profile_rows": ex.orphan_profile_rows,
            "out_of_range_profile_rows": ex.out_of_range_profile_rows,
            "duplicate_profile_rows": ex.duplicate_profile_rows,
            "unknown_region_site_rows": ex.unknown_region_site_rows,
            "waiver_signed_missing": ex.waiver_signed_missing,
            "ineligible_offer_count": stats["ineligible_offer_count"],
            "allowance_blocked_offer_count": 
stats["allowance_blocked_offer_count"],
            "empty_candidate_groups": stats["empty_candidate_groups"],
            "zero_floor_count": zero_floor,
            "unallocated_pool_count": stats["unallocated_pool_count"],
        }
    
    
    def policy_audit_document(labels, stats, tie_decisions):
        counts = dict.fromkeys(LABELS, 0)
        site_labels = []
        for key in sorted(labels):
            counts[labels[key]] += 1
            site_labels.append({"event_id": key[0], "site_id": key[1], "label": 
labels[key]})
        return {
            "compliance_counts": counts,
            "site_labels": site_labels,
            "tie_decisions": sorted(tie_decisions, key=lambda d: (d["event_id"],
d["region"])),
            "cap_binding_count": stats["cap_binding_count"],
            "residual_cents": stats["residual_cents"],
            "residual_tie_pool_count": stats["residual_tie_pool_count"],
        }
    
    
    def write_json(out_dir, name, document):
        with open(os.path.join(out_dir, name), "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            json.dump(document, fh, sort_keys=True, indent=1)
            fh.write("\n")
    
    
    def main(argv):
        if len(argv) != 3:
            print("usage: derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR", file=sys.stderr)
            return 2
        data_dir, out_dir = argv[1], argv[2]
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
        ex = Extract(data_dir)
        labels, results, stats, tie_decisions = run(ex)
    
        region_docs = {region: region_document(ex, results, region) for region 
in ex.region_names}
        segment_docs = {seg: segment_document(ex, results, seg) for seg in 
ex.segment_names}
        for region, doc in region_docs.items():
            write_json(out_dir, "region_%s.json" % region, doc)
        for segment, doc in segment_docs.items():
            write_json(out_dir, "segment_%s.json" % segment, doc)
        write_json(out_dir, "summary.json", summary_document(ex, region_docs, 
segment_docs))
        write_json(out_dir, "policy_audit.json", policy_audit_document(labels, 
stats, tie_decisions))
        write_json(out_dir, "data_quality.json", data_quality_document(ex, 
stats))
        return 0
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

+108|                "interval_count": int(ev["interval_count"]),
+109|                "cost_weight": int(ev["objective"]["cost_weight"]),
+110|                "recovery_weight": int(ev["objective"]["recovery_weight"]),
+111|                "uplift": int(ev["uplift_millicents_per_kwh"]),
+112|                "start": datetime.fromisoformat(ev["dispatch_start"]),
+113|            }
+114|        self.event_ids_sorted = sorted(self.events)
+115|        self.season_order = sorted(
+116|            self.events.values(), key=lambda e: (e["start"], 
NegStr(e["event_id"]))
+117|        )

+158|    def load_profiles(self):
+159|        self.profiles_rows = 0
+160|        self.orphan_profile_rows = 0
+161|        self.out_of_range_profile_rows = 0
+162|        self.duplicate_profile_rows = 0
+163|        relief = {}
+164|        recovery = {}
+165|        seen = set()
+166|        with open(self.path("profiles.tsv"), encoding="utf-8", newline="")

+167|            for row in csv.DictReader(fh, delimiter="\t"):
+168|                self.profiles_rows += 1
+169|                offer_id = row["offer_id"]
+170|                event_id = self.offer_events.get(offer_id)
+171|                if event_id is None:
+172|                    self.orphan_profile_rows += 1
+173|                    continue

+175|                if interval < 1 or interval > 
self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]:
+176|                    self.out_of_range_profile_rows += 1
+177|                    continue
+178|                key = (offer_id, interval)
+179|                if key in seen:
+180|                    self.duplicate_profile_rows += 1
+181|                    continue
+182|                seen.add(key)
+183|                if offer_id not in self.offers:
+184|                    continue
+185|                mult = int(row["register_multiplier"])
+186|                relief[key] = self.ticks(int(row["relief_wh"]) * mult)
+187|                recovery[key] = self.ticks(int(row["recovery_wh"]) * mult)
+188|        for offer in self.offers.values():
+189|            offer_id = offer["offer_id"]
+190|            count = self.events[offer["event_id"]]["interval_count"]
+191|            offer["relief"] = [relief.get((offer_id, t), 0) for t in 
range(1, count + 1)]
+192|            offer["recovery"] = [recovery.get((offer_id, t), 0) for t in 
range(1, count + 1)]
+193|            offer["total_relief"] = sum(offer["relief"])
+194|            offer["total_recovery"] = sum(offer["recovery"])

+302|    def compliance_label(self, key):
+303|        state = self.state.get(key, {})
+304|        if state.get("safety_hold"):
+305|            return "SAFETY_HOLD"
+306|        if state.get("cooldown"):
+307|            return "COOLDOWN"
+308|        waiver = self.waivers.get(key, {})
+309|        if waiver.get("routine_certificate_pending") != "Y":
+310|            return "CLEAR"
+311|        site = self.site_rows[key]
+312|        valid = (
+313|            waiver.get("waiver_status") == "VALID"
+314|            and waiver.get("waiver_signed") == "Y"
+315|            and site["critical"]
+316|            and site["segment"] == "public"
+317|        )
+318|        return "CONTINUITY_WAIVED" if valid else "CERTIFICATE_PENDING"
+319|
+320|    def rates(self, key):
+321|        """Return (pre-cap rate, effective rate, cap binding) for a site 
row."""
+322|        start, cap = self.cards[key]
+323|        site = self.site_rows[key]
+324|        pre_cap = start + self.events[site["event_id"]]["uplift"] + 
self.segment_adder[site["segment"]]
+325|        return pre_cap, min(pre_cap, cap), pre_cap > cap
+326|
+327|    def credit_cents(self, offer, rate):
+328|        den = 1000 * self.millicents_per_cent
+329|        total = 0
+330|        for relief, recovery in zip(offer["relief"], offer["recovery"]):
+331|            credited = relief - recovery
+332|            if credited > 0:
+333|                total += round_half_even(credited * self.wh_per_tick * 
rate, den)
+334|        return total
+335|
+336|
+337|def solve_portfolio(ex, event, region, site_groups):
+338|    """Return (best portfolio offer ids, number of portfolios tied at the 
best value)."""
+339|    count = event["interval_count"]
+340|    event_id = event["event_id"]
+341|    targets = []
+342|    weights = []
+343|    relief_cap = []
+344|    recovery_cap = []
+345|    for t in range(1, count + 1):
+346|        target, weight = ex.targets.get((event_id, region, t), (0, 0))
+347|        targets.append(target)
+348|        weights.append(weight)
+349|        rcap, vcap = ex.ceilings.get((event_id, region, t), (0, 0))
+350|        relief_cap.append(rcap)
+351|        recovery_cap.append(vcap)
+352|    floors = []
+353|    for segment in ex.segment_names:
+354|        floor, priority = ex.floors.get((event_id, region, segment), (0, 
0))
+355|        if floor > 0 and priority > 0:
+356|            floors.append((segment, floor, priority))
+357|    cost_w = event["cost_weight"]
+358|    rec_w = event["recovery_weight"]
+359|
+360|    group_offers = [offers for _, offers in sorted(site_groups.items())]
+361|    n = len(group_offers)
+362|    relief = [0] * count
+363|    recovery = [0] * count
+364|    seg_relief = {segment: 0 for segment in ex.segment_names}
+365|    chosen = []
+366|    best_key = None
+367|    best_ids = None
+368|    tied = 0
+369|
+370|    def leaf(cost):
+371|        nonlocal best_key, best_ids, tied
+372|        shortfall = 0
+373|        over = 0
+374|        recovery_total = 0
+375|        for t in range(count):
+376|            diff = targets[t] - relief[t]
+377|            if diff > 0:
+378|                shortfall += weights[t] * diff
+379|            else:
+380|                over -= diff
+381|            recovery_total += recovery[t]
+382|        deficit = 0
+383|        for segment, floor, priority in floors:
+384|            gap = floor - seg_relief[segment]
+385|            if gap > 0:
+386|                deficit += priority * gap
+387|        key = (shortfall, deficit, cost_w * cost + rec_w * recovery_total, 
over, len(chosen))
+388|        if best_key is None or key < best_key:
+389|            best_key = key
+390|            best_ids = tuple(sorted(chosen))
+391|            tied = 1
+392|        elif key == best_key:
+393|            tied += 1
+394|            ids = tuple(sorted(chosen))
+395|            if ids > best_ids:
+396|                best_ids = ids
+397|
+398|    def walk(index, cost):
+399|        if index == n:
+400|            leaf(cost)
+401|            return
+402|        walk(index + 1, cost)
+403|        for offer in group_offers[index]:
+404|            orelief = offer["relief"]
+405|            orecovery = offer["recovery"]
+406|            feasible = True
+407|            for t in range(count):
+408|                if (
+409|                    relief[t] + orelief[t] > relief_cap[t]
+410|                    or recovery[t] + orecovery[t] > recovery_cap[t]
+411|                ):
+412|                    feasible = False
+413|                    break
+414|            if not feasible:
+415|                continue
+416|            for t in range(count):
+417|                relief[t] += orelief[t]
+418|                recovery[t] += orecovery[t]
+419|            seg_relief[offer["segment"]] += offer["total_relief"]
+420|            chosen.append(offer["offer_id"])
+421|            walk(index + 1, cost + offer["bid_cost_cents"])
+422|            chosen.pop()
+423|            seg_relief[offer["segment"]] -= offer["total_relief"]
+424|            for t in range(count):
+425|                relief[t] -= orelief[t]
+426|                recovery[t] -= orecovery[t]
+427|
+428|    walk(0, 0)
+429|    return best_ids, tied
+430|
+431|
+432|def largest_remainder(funded, weights):
+433|    """Share `funded` cents by largest remainder. Returns (shares, 
residual, tie)."""
+434|    total_weight = sum(weights.values())
+435|    shares = {}
+436|    remainders = {}
+437|    for beneficiary, weight in weights.items():
+438|        shares[beneficiary], remainders[beneficiary] = divmod(funded * 
weight, total_weight)
+439|    leftover = funded - sum(shares.values())
+440|    order = sorted(remainders, key=lambda b: (remainders[b], b), 
reverse=True)
+441|    for beneficiary in order[:leftover]:
+442|        shares[beneficiary] += 1
+443|    seen = set()
+444|    tie = False
+445|    for value in remainders.values():
+446|        if value:
+447|            if value in seen:
+448|                tie = True
+449|                break
+450|            seen.add(value)
+451|    return shares, leftover, tie
+452|
+453|
+454|def apportion(ex, funded, weights):
+455|    """Share a funded pool among beneficiaries. Returns (shares, residual 
cents, tie)."""
+456|    positive = {b: w for b, w in weights.items() if w > 0}
+457|    if not positive:
+458|        return {}, 0, False
+459|    claims = {b: w * ex.claim_cents_per_weight for b, w in 
positive.items()}
+460|    if sum(claims.values()) <= funded:
+461|        return claims, 0, False
+462|    shares, residual, tie = largest_remainder(funded, positive)
+463|    kept = {b: w for b, w in positive.items() if shares[b] >= 
ex.minimum_grant_cents}
+464|    if not kept:
+465|        return {b: 0 for b in positive}, 0, False
+466|    if len(kept) == len(positive):
+467|        return shares, residual, tie
+468|    second, residual, tie = largest_remainder(funded, kept)
+469|    return {b: second.get(b, 0) for b in positive}, residual, tie
+470|
+471|
+472|def run(ex):
+473|    labels = {key: ex.compliance_label(key) for key in ex.site_rows}
+474|
+475|    offers_by_event = {}
+476|    for offer in ex.offers.values():
+477|        offers_by_event.setdefault(offer["event_id"], []).append(offer)
+478|    site_rows_by_event_region = {}
+479|    for key, site in ex.site_rows.items():
+480|        site_rows_by_event_region.setdefault((site["event_id"], 
site["region"]), []).append(key)
+481|
+482|    stats = {
+483|        "ineligible_offer_count": 0,
+484|        "allowance_blocked_offer_count": 0,
+485|        "empty_candidate_groups": 0,
+486|        "cap_binding_count": 0,
+487|        "residual_cents": 0,
+488|        "residual_tie_pool_count": 0,
+489|        "unallocated_pool_count": 0,
+490|    }
+491|    consumed = {}
+492|    carried = {region: 0 for region in ex.region_names}
+493|    results = {}
+494|    tie_decisions = []
+495|
+496|    for event in ex.season_order:
+497|        event_id = event["event_id"]
+498|        count = event["interval_count"]
+499|        candidates = {}
+500|        for offer in offers_by_event.get(event_id, []):
+501|            key = (event_id, offer["site_id"])
+502|            if not ex.state.get(key, {}).get("dispatch_authorized"):
+503|                stats["ineligible_offer_count"] += 1
+504|                continue
+505|            root = ex.allowance_root[offer["site_id"]]
+506|            if offer["total_relief"] > ex.allowance_ticks[root] - 
consumed.get(root, 0):
+507|                stats["allowance_blocked_offer_count"] += 1
+508|                continue
+509|            region = ex.site_rows[key]["region"]
+510|            candidates.setdefault(region, {}).setdefault(offer["site_id"], 
[]).append(offer)
+511|
+512|        for region in ex.region_names:
+513|            site_groups = candidates.get(region, {})
+514|            candidate_count = sum(len(group) for group in 
site_groups.values())
+515|            if candidate_count == 0:
+516|                stats["empty_candidate_groups"] += 1
+517|            selected_ids, tied = solve_portfolio(ex, event, region, 
site_groups)
+518|            if tied >= 2:
+519|                tie_decisions.append(
+520|                    {
+521|                        "event_id": event_id,
+522|                        "region": region,
+523|                        "tied_portfolio_count": tied,
+524|                        "selected_offer_ids": list(selected_ids),
+525|                    }
+526|                )
+527|
+528|            relief = [0] * count
+529|            recovery = [0] * count
+530|            bid_cost = 0
+531|            credits = 0
+532|            offer_credits = {}
+533|            for offer_id in selected_ids:
+534|                offer = ex.offers[offer_id]
+535|                key = (event_id, offer["site_id"])
+536|                _, rate, binding = ex.rates(key)
+537|                if binding:
+538|                    stats["cap_binding_count"] += 1
+539|                offer_credits[offer_id] = ex.credit_cents(offer, rate)
+540|                credits += offer_credits[offer_id]
+541|                bid_cost += offer["bid_cost_cents"]
+542|                for t in range(count):
+543|                    relief[t] += offer["relief"][t]
+544|                    recovery[t] += offer["recovery"][t]
+545|                root = ex.allowance_root[offer["site_id"]]
+546|                consumed[root] = consumed.get(root, 0) + 
offer["total_relief"]
+547|
+548|            intervals = []
+549|            for t in range(count):
+550|                target = ex.targets.get((event_id, region, t + 1), (0, 
0))[0]
+551|                intervals.append(
+552|                    {
+553|                        "interval": t + 1,
+554|                        "target_ticks": target,
+555|                        "relief_ticks": relief[t],
+556|                        "recovery_ticks": recovery[t],
+557|                        "shortfall_ticks": max(0, target - relief[t]),
+558|                        "overdelivery_ticks": max(0, relief[t] - target),
+559|                    }
+560|                )
+561|
+562|            pool = ex.pools.get((event_id, region), 0)
+563|            funded = max(0, pool + carried[region] - credits)
+564|            site_keys = site_rows_by_event_region.get((event_id, region), 
[])
+565|            weights = {}
+566|            recipients = 0
+567|            for key in site_keys:
+568|                if key not in ex.grant_weights:
+569|                    continue
+570|                recipients += 1
+571|                weight = ex.grant_weights[key]
+572|                if labels[key] == "CONTINUITY_WAIVED":
+573|                    weight *= 2
+574|                beneficiary = ex.site_rows[key]["beneficiary_id"]
+575|                weights[beneficiary] = weights.get(beneficiary, 0) + weight
+576|            if recipients == 0 or sum(weights.values()) == 0:
+577|                stats["unallocated_pool_count"] += 1
+578|                shares, residual, tie = {}, 0, False
+579|            else:
+580|                shares, residual, tie = apportion(ex, funded, weights)
+581|            stats["residual_cents"] += residual
+582|            if tie:
+583|                stats["residual_tie_pool_count"] += 1
+584|            allocated = sum(shares.values())
+585|            carried[region] = funded - allocated
+586|
+587|            counts = {label: 0 for label in LABELS}
+588|            for key in site_keys:
+589|                counts[labels[key]] += 1
+590|
+591|            results[(event_id, region)] = {
+592|                "candidate_offer_count": candidate_count,
+593|                "selected_offer_ids": list(selected_ids),
+594|                "intervals": intervals,
+595|                "bid_cost_cents": bid_cost,
+596|                "credit_cents": credits,
+597|                "grant_pool_cents": pool,
+598|                "grant_funded_cents": funded,
+599|                "grant_allocated_cents": allocated,
+600|                "grant_unallocated_cents": funded - allocated,
+601|                "grants": [{"beneficiary_id": b, "cents": shares[b]} for b 
in sorted(shares)],
+602|                "compliance_counts": counts,
+603|                "offer_credits": offer_credits,
+604|            }
+605|
+606|    return labels, results, stats, tie_decisions
+607|
+608|
+609|REGION_EVENT_FIELDS = (
+610|    "candidate_offer_count",
+611|    "selected_offer_ids",
+612|    "intervals",
+613|    "bid_cost_cents",
+614|    "credit_cents",
+615|    "grant_pool_cents",
+616|    "grant_funded_cents",
+617|    "grant_allocated_cents",
+618|    "grant_unallocated_cents",
+619|    "grants",
+620|    "compliance_counts",
+621|)
+622|
+623|REGION_MONEY_FIELDS = (
+624|    "bid_cost_cents",

+633|def region_document(ex, results, region):
+634|    events = []
+635|    totals = dict.fromkeys(
+636|        (
+637|            "event_count",
+638|            "candidate_offer_count",
+639|            "selected_offer_count",
+640|            "relief_ticks",
+641|            "recovery_ticks",
+642|            "shortfall_ticks",
+643|            "overdelivery_ticks",

+646|        0,
+647|    )

+649|        res = results[(event_id, region)]

+651|        for field in REGION_EVENT_FIELDS:
+652|            entry[field] = res[field]
+653|        events.append(entry)
+654|        totals["event_count"] += 1
+655|        totals["candidate_offer_count"] += res["candidate_offer_count"]

+657|        for interval in res["intervals"]:
+658|            for field in (
+659|                "relief_ticks",
+660|                "recovery_ticks",
+661|                "shortfall_ticks",
+662|                "overdelivery_ticks",
+663|            ):
+664|                totals[field] += interval[field]
+665|        for field in REGION_MONEY_FIELDS:
+666|            totals[field] += res[field]
+667|    return {"region": region, "events": events, "totals": totals}

+670|def segment_document(ex, results, segment):
+671|    entries = []
+672|    totals = dict.fromkeys(
+673|        (
+674|            "selected_offer_count",
+675|            "relief_ticks",
+676|            "recovery_ticks",
+677|            "bid_cost_cents",

+681|        0,
+682|    )

+684|        for region in ex.region_names:
+685|            res = results[(event_id, region)]
+686|            offer_ids = []
+687|            relief = 0
+688|            recovery = 0
+689|            bid_cost = 0
+690|            credits = 0
+691|            for offer_id in res["selected_offer_ids"]:
+692|                offer = ex.offers[offer_id]
+693|                if offer["segment"] != segment:
+694|                    continue
+695|                offer_ids.append(offer_id)
+696|                relief += offer["total_relief"]
+697|                recovery += offer["total_recovery"]
+698|                bid_cost += offer["bid_cost_cents"]
+699|                credits += res["offer_credits"][offer_id]
+700|            floor = ex.floors.get((event_id, region, segment), (0, 0))[0]
+701|            attainment = 10000 if floor == 0 else min(10000, 
round_half_up(10000 * relief, floor))
+702|            entries.append(
+703|                {
+704|                    "event_id": event_id,
+705|                    "region": region,
+706|                    "selected_offer_ids": offer_ids,
+707|                    "relief_ticks": relief,
+708|                    "recovery_ticks": recovery,
+709|                    "bid_cost_cents": bid_cost,
+710|                    "credit_cents": credits,
+711|                    "floor_ticks": floor,
+712|                    "floor_attainment_bp": attainment,
+713|                }
+714|            )
+715|            totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(offer_ids)
+716|            totals["relief_ticks"] += relief
+717|            totals["recovery_ticks"] += recovery
+718|            totals["bid_cost_cents"] += bid_cost
+719|            totals["credit_cents"] += credits
+720|            totals["floor_ticks"] += floor
+721|    return {"segment": segment, "event_regions": entries, "totals": totals}

independent brute-force check on a sample of groups. I also want to measure how 
often near-ties occur — portfolios matching on the first four components but not
the count — to make sure my tie logic isn't just failing to find real ties 
rather than correctly finding none.

Now I'm going to compute, for every event-region, how many feasible portfolios 
exist and whether any tie on the successive prefixes of ranking components to 
see where real ties might emerge.



$ cd /app && python3 -c "
import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'out')
import derive_report as dr, collections
ex=dr.Extract('data')

def enumerate_keys(event, region, groups):
    count=event['interval_count']; eid=event['event_id']
    tg=[ex.targets.get((eid,region,t),(0,0)) for t in range(1,count+1)]
    ce=[ex.ceilings.get((eid,region,t),(0,0)) for t in range(1,count+1)]
    floors=[(s,)+ex.floors.get((eid,region,s),(0,0)) for s in ex.segment_names]
    res=[]
    glist=[v for k,v in sorted(groups.items())]
    def walk(i, sel):
        if i==len(glist):
            rel=[0]*count; rec=[0]*count; segrel=collections.Counter(); cost=0
            for o in sel:
                for t in range(count): rel[t]+=o['relief'][t]; 
rec[t]+=o['recovery'][t]
                segrel[o['segment']]+=o['total_relief']; 
cost+=o['bid_cost_cents']
            for t in range(count):
                if rel[t]>ce[t][0] or rec[t]>ce[t][1]: return
            sh=sum(tg[t][1]*max(0,tg[t][0]-rel[t]) for t in range(count))
            ov=sum(max(0,rel[t]-tg[t][0]) for t in range(count))
            df=sum(p*max(0,f-segrel[s]) for s,f,p in floors)
            bl=event['cost_weight']*cost+event['recovery_weight']*sum(rec)
            res.append((sh,df,bl,ov,len(sel)))
            return
        walk(i+1, sel)
        for o in glist[i]: walk(i+1, sel+[o])
    walk(0,[])
    return res

# replicate candidate selection quickly (ignoring allowance for diagnostics? no,
use full run order)
labels, results, stats, ties = dr.run(ex)
print('ties', len(ties))

def enumerate_keys(event, region, groups):
    count=event['interval_count']; eid=event['event_id']
    tg=[ex.targets.get((eid,region,t),(0,0)) for t in range(1,count+1)]
    ce=[ex.ceilings.get((eid,region,t),(0,0)) for t in range(1,count+1)]

    res=[]
    glist=[v for k,v in sorted(groups.items())]
    def walk(i, sel):
        if i==len(glist):
            rel=[0]*count; rec=[0]*count; segrel=collections.Counter(); cost=0
            for o in sel:
                for t in range(count): rel[t]+=o['relief'][t]; 
rec[t]+=o['recovery'][t]
                segrel[o['segment']]+=o['total_relief']; 
cost+=o['bid_cost_cents']
            for t in range(count):
                if rel[t]>ce[t][0] or rec[t]>ce[t][1]: return
            sh=sum(tg[t][1]*max(0,tg[t][0]-rel[t]) for t in range(count))
            ov=sum(max(0,rel[t]-tg[t][0]) for t in range(count))
            df=sum(p*max(0,f-segrel[s]) for s,f,p in floors)
            bl=event['cost_weight']*cost+event['recovery_weight']*sum(rec)
            res.append((sh,df,bl,ov,len(sel)))
            return
        walk(i+1, sel)
        for o in glist[i]: walk(i+1, sel+[o])
    walk(0,[])
    return res

import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'out')
import derive_report as dr, collections
ex=dr.Extract('data')
stats=collections.Counter()
orig=dr.solve_portfolio
def patched(ex2, event, region, groups):
    count=event['interval_count']; eid=event['event_id']
    tg=[ex.targets.get((eid,region,t),(0,0)) for t in range(1,count+1)]
    ce=[ex.ceilings.get((eid,region,t),(0,0)) for t in range(1,count+1)]
    floors=[(s,)+ex.floors.get((eid,region,s),(0,0)) for s in ex.segment_names]
    keys=[]
    glist=[v for k,v in sorted(groups.items())]
    def walk(i, sel):
        if i==len(glist):
            rel=[0]*count; rec=[0]*count; segrel=collections.Counter(); cost=0
            for o in sel:
                for t in range(count): rel[t]+=o['relief'][t]; 
rec[t]+=o['recovery'][t]
                segrel[o['segment']]+=o['total_relief']; 
cost+=o['bid_cost_cents']
            for t in range(count):
                if rel[t]>ce[t][0] or rec[t]>ce[t][1]: return
            sh=sum(tg[t][1]*max(0,tg[t][0]-rel[t]) for t in range(count))
            ov=sum(max(0,rel[t]-tg[t][0]) for t in range(count))
            df=sum(p*max(0,f-segrel[s]) for s,f,p in floors)
            bl=event['cost_weight']*cost+event['recovery_weight']*sum(rec)
            keys.append((sh,df,bl,ov,len(sel)))
            return
        walk(i+1, sel)
        for o in glist[i]: walk(i+1, sel+[o])
    walk(0,[])
    stats['groups']+=1
    stats['portfolios']+=len(keys)
    for n in (1,2,3,4,5):
        pre=[k[:n] for k in keys]
        best=min(pre)
        if pre.count(best)>1: stats['tie%d'%n]+=1
    return orig(ex2,event,region,groups)
dr.solve_portfolio=patched
dr.run(ex)
